1b1('bum): @##//shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 
1b2('bum): stong phrag brgya pa/bam po nyis brgya 
1b3('bum): zhe bdun pa'o//gzugs dben pa'i phyir 
2a1('bum): @##//shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dben pa'o//tshor ba 
2a2('bum): dben pa'i phyir shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin/pa dben 
2a3('bum): pa'o 'du shes dben pa'i phyir shes rabkyi pha rol 
2a4('bum): tu phyin pa dben pa'o//'du byed dben pa'i phyir shes rabakyi pha rol 
2b1('bum): tu phyin pa dben pa'o//rnam par shes pa dben pa'i phyir shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa
2b2('bum): dben pa'o//mig dben pa'i phyir shes rabagyi pha rol tu phyin pa dben pa'o/rna ba dben pa'i phyir she
2b3('bum): rab kyi pha rol tu phyin na pa dben pa'o//sna dben pa'i phyir shes ra ba kyi pha rol tu phyin
2b4('bum): pa dben pa'o//lce dben pa'i phyir shes rang kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dbenapa'//lus dben pa'i phyir 
2b5('bum): shes rab kyi pha rol tu byin pa dben pa'o//yid bden pa'i phyir shes rab kyi pharol tu phyin pa dben pa'i
3a1('bum): @##/7/gzugs dben pa'i phyir shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dben pa'o//sgra 
3a2('bum): dben pa'i phyir shes rab gyi pha rol tu phyin pa dbenapa'o//dri dben pa'i phyir shes rabakyi pha rol tu phyin 
3a3('bum): pa dben pa'o//ro dben pa'i phyir shes rabagyi pha rol tu phyin pa dbe napa'o//reg dben 
3a4('bum): pa'i phyir shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dben pa 'o//chos dben pa'i phyir shes 
3a5('bum): kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dbeg pa'o//mig gi rnam par shes pa dben pa'i phyir shes rabkyi pha rol 
3a6('bum): tu phyin pa dben pa'o/rna ba'i rnam par shes pa dbe na pa'i phyir shes rab kyi pa rol tu phyin pa 
3b1('bum): dben pa'o//sna'i rnam par shes pa dben pa'i phyir shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'o//lce'i rnam par shes pa dbenapa'i 
3b2('bum): phyir/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyinapa dben pa'o//lus kyi rnam par shes pa dben pa'i phyir shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin 
3b3('bum): pa dben pa'o//yid kyi rnam par shes pa dbe pa'i phyir shes rab kyi pha  rol tu phyin pa dben pa'o//mig gi 'dus te reg pa dben 
3b4('bum): pa'i phyir shes rab kyi pha raul tu phyin pa dben pa'o //rna ba'i 'dus te reg pa dben pa'i phyir shes rab gyi pha rol tu 
3b5('bum): phyin pa dben pa'o//sna'i 'dus te reg pa dbenapa'i phyir shes rab kyi pha ril tu phyin pa dben pa'o//lce'i 'dus te reg pa dbe pa'i phyir 
3b6('bum): shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyinas pa dben pa'o//lus (kyi )'dus te reg pa dben pa'i phyir shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dben
3b7('bum): pa'o/yid kyi 'dus te reg pa dbend pa'i phyir shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dben pa'o/mig gi 'dus te reg pa las 
4a1('bum): @##//byung ba'i tshor ba dben pa'i phyir shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dben pa'o//rna ba'i 'dus te reg pa las byung ba'i 
4a2('bum): tshor ba dben pa'i phyir shas rab kyi pha rol tu phyinapa dben pa'o//sna'i 'dus te reg pa las byu ba'i tshor ba dben pa'i phyer shes rab 
4a3('bum): kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dbe pa'o//lce'i 'dus te reg pa las byang ba'i tshor ba dben pa 'i phyir/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi pa dben pa'o//
4a4('bum): lus kyi 'dus te reg pa las byungaba'i tshor ba dben pa'i phir/shes rab kyi pha rol/tu phyin pa dben pa'o/ /yid kyi 'dus 
4a5('bum): te reg pa las byung ba'i tshor ba dben pa'o/phyir/shes rab kyi pha rol1tu phyin pa dben pa'o/sa'i khams dben pa'i phyir/
4a6('bum): shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyinas pa dben pa'o//chu'i khams dben pa'i phir/shes rib kyi pha rol tu phyin pa deben pa'o//me'i khams dben 
4a7('bum): pa'i phyir/shes rab kyi pha ral tu phyin pa dben pa'o//rlung gi khams dben pa'i phyir/shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa dben pa'o/nam 
4b1('bum): mkha'i khams dben pa'i phyir/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dben pa'o//rnam par shes pa'i khams dben pa'i phyir/shes rab kyi pha rol tu pyin pa dben pa'o/
4b2('bum): ma rig pa dben pa'i phyir/shes rib kyi pha rol tu phyin pa deben pa'o//'du byed dben pa'i phyir/shes ba rig pa rig pa 'di 'di ni rig pa 'di ni bdag dang shes 
4b3('bum): pa dben pa'i phyir/she rab kyi pha rol tu phyin dben pa'o//ming dang gzugs dben pa'i phyir/sher rab kyi pha rol tu phyin na dben pa'o//rnam par
4b4('bum): drug dben pa'i phyir/shes pa rig pa rig pa rig pa 'di ni yin no//rig dben pa'i phyir/she' rab kyi pha rol tu phyin ni dben pa'o//skye mched
4b5('bum): dben pa'i phyir/shes myi pha rol tu phyin pa dbe na pa'o//sred pa dben pa'i phyir/shis rab kyi pha rol tu phyin ga dben pa'o//tshor ba
4b6('bum): phyir/shes rab kyi pha ral tu phyin pa dben pa'o//srid pa dben pa'i phyir/shes rab kyi pha ral du phyin pa dben pa'o/skye ba dben pa'i phyir/shes
4b7('bum): rol tu phyin pa dben pa'o//shi dben pa'i phyir/shes rib kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dben po'i sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa dbeg pa'i phyir/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin
4b8('bum): tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin pa deben pa'i phyir/shes rab gyi pha rol tu phyin pa dbeb pa'o//bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa deb pa'i phyir/shes rab kyis pha rol tu
5a1('bum): @#//phyin pa dben pa'o//brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dben pa'i phyir/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dbain pa'o//bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa dben
5a2('bum): pa'i phyir/shes rab kyi pha ral tu phyin pa dben pa'o//nang stong pa nyid dben pa'i phyir/shes rang kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dang dben pa'o//phyi stong pa nyid dben pa'i phyer/shes rab kyi
5a3('bum): pha rol tu phyin pa dben ba'o//phyi nang stong pa nyid dben ba'i phyir/shes rab kyi pha ril tu phyin pa dben pa'o//stong pa nyid stong pa nyid dben pa'i pyir/shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin
5a4('bum): pa dben pa'o//chen po stong pa nyid dben pa'i phir/shes rab kyi pha rol rtu phyin pa dben pa'o/don dam pa stong pa nyid dben pa'i phyor/shes rab kyi pha rol ta
5a5('bum): phyin pa dben pa'o//'dus byas stong pa nyid dben pa'i/phyir/shes rab kyi pha rolatu phyin pa dben pa'o/'dus ma byas stong pa nyid dbenapa'i phyir/shes rab kyi
5a6('bum): pha rolatu phyin pa dben ba'o//mtha' las 'das pa stong pa nyid dben pa'o phyir/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dbenapa'o/thog ma dang tha ma med pa stong pa nyid dben ba'i
5a7('bum): shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyinapa dben pa'o//dor ba med pa stong pa nyid dbe pa'i phyir/shes rab kyi pha ral tu phyin pa dben pa'o rang bzhin stong pa nyid dben pa'i phyir/shes
5a8('bum): rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i dben pa'o//chos thams cad stong pa nyid dben pa'i phyer/shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa dben pa'o//rang gi mtshan nyid stong pa nyid dben pa' phyir/
5b1('bum): shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dben pa'o//mi dmigs pa stong pa nyid dben pa'i phyir/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyinapa dben pa'o//dngos po med pa stong pa nyid dben pa' 
5b2('bum): phyir;shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi pa dben pa'o//ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid dben pa'i phy-ir/shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa dben pa'o//dngos pa'i med pa'i ngo bo nyid stong
5b3('bum): pa nyid dben pa'i phyir/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin:pa dben pa'o//dran pa nye bar gzhag pa dben pa'i phyir/shes rang kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dpen pa'o//yang dag par spong ba
5b4('bum): dben pa'i phyir/shes rabakyi pha rol tu phyin pa dbain ba'o//rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa dben pa'i phyir/she rab kyi pha rol tu phyin 'a dben pa'o//dbang po dben pa'i phyir;
5b5('bum): shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyinas pa dben pa'o//stobs dben pa'i phyir/shes rapa kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dben pa'o//byang chub kyi yan lag dben pa'i phyir/shes parab kyi pha
5b6('bum): rol tu phyin pa dben pa'i//'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad pa dben pa'i phyir/sher rab kyi pha rol tu phyinapa dben pa'o//'phags pa'i bden pa dben pa'i phyir/she
5b7('bum): rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dpen pa'o//bsam gtan dben pa'i phyir/shes rib kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'daben pa'o//tshad med pa dben pa'i phyir/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi pa dben pa'o//
5b8('bum): gzugs med pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa dben pa'i phyir/shes rang kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dang ben pa'o//rnam par thar pa dben pa'i phyir/shesarab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i dben pa'o//mthar
6a1('bum): @#//gyis gnas pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa dben pa'i phyir/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dben pa'o//rnam par thar pa'i sgo stong pa nyid dang /mtshan ma med pa dang 
6a2('bum): smon pa med pa dben pa'i phyir/shesarab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa bden pa'o//mngon par shes pa dben pa'i phyir/she rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pas dben pa'o//ting nge 'dzin dben pa'i phyir 
6a3('bum): shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi pa dben pa'o//gzungs kyi sgo dben pa'i phyir/shes rib kyi pha rol tu phyin pa deben pa'o//de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs dben pa'i phyir/shes yi rab kyi 
6a4('bum): pha rol tu phyin pa dbenapa'o//mi 'jigs pa dben pa'i phyir/ra/shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa dben pa'o//so so yang dag par rig pa dben pa'i phyir/zhes rab kyi pha rol 
6a5('bum): tu phyin pa dben pa'o/snying rje chen po dben pa'i phyir/shes rag kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dbi+en pa'o//sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa dben pa'i phyir/shis rab kyi pha 
6a6('bum): rol tu phyin pa dben pa'o//rgyun tu zhugs pa'i 'bras bu dben pa'i phyir/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin dben pa'o//lan cig phyir 'ong ba'i 'bras bu dben pa'i pyir/shes rab kyi 
6a7('bum): pha rol tu phyin dben pa dben pa'o//phyir mi 'ong ba'i 'bras bu dbe pa'i phyir/shes rab kyi phar rol tu phyin pa dben pa dang /dgra bcom pa nyid dben pa'i phyir/shes rib kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 
6a8('bum): dben pa'o//rang byang chub dben pa'i phyir/shes dab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa deben pa'o//lam gyi rnam pa shes pa nyid dben pa'i phyir shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi pa dben pa'o//rnam pa 
6b1('bum): thams cad mkhyen pa nyid dben pa'i phyir/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dben pa'o//lha'i bu dag gal te/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po chos thams cad dben par
6b2('bum): bshad pa la sems mi 'jum pa yongs su zhum par mi 'gyur la/de'i yid 'jigs par mi 'gyur na/byang chub sems dpa' ses dpa' chen po shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi na pa la
6b3('bum): spyod pa'o//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis/tshe dang ldan pa rab 'byor la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//rab 'byor ci'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' sems/dpa' chen po shes rab gyi pha rol
6b4('bum): tu phyin pa zhum par mi 'gyur ba yin/gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das chos thams cad ma mchis pa'i slad du/byang chub sems dpa' chen po shes rab kyang pha rol tu phyin
6b5('bum): pa la zhum par mi 'gyuro//bcom ldan 'das chos thams/cad dben pa dang /zhi ba dang /ma skyes pa'i slad du/byang chub
6b6('bum): rol tu phyin pa la zhum par mi 'gyur te/bcom ldan 'das de'i slad du/byang chub sem dpa' sems dpa' chen po shes rib kyi pha
6b7('bum): bcom ldan 'das de ni 'di ltar gang zhum par 'gyur ba dang /gang gis zhum par 'gyur ba dang gang la zhum par 'gyur ba'i chos de dag thams cad ni ma mchis pa ste/dmigs su ma mchiso//
6b8('bum): bcom ldan 'das gal te/byang chub sems dpa' sem dpa' chen po de skad du bshad pa la mi 'jum/mi 'gong zhing /yid chad pa dang /sgyid lugs par mi 'gyur la/mi 'jigs mi sngang
7a1('bum): @#//skrag par mi 'gyur na/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod pa lags so//de ci'i slad du zhe na/bcom ldan 'das de ni 'di ltar gang zhum par 'gyur ba 'am/gang 
7a2('bum): gis zhum par 'gyur ba 'am/gang la zhum par 'gyur ba'i chos de dag thams cad ni ma mchis te/bcom ldan 'das de ltar spyod pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po ni/dbang 
7a3('bum): po dang bcas/tshangs pa dang bcas/skye dgu'i bdag po dang bcas pa'i lha rnams kyis phyag bgyi bar 'gyuro//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/rab 'byor byang chub sems dpa' 
7a4('bum): sems dpa' chen po gang shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyinapa la spyod pa de ni/dbang po dang bcas/tshangs pa dag bcas pa/skye dgu'i bdag po dang dang bcas pa'i lha rnams gyis phyag bya bar ma zad do/
7a5('bum): gang 'phags pa mngon par 'phags pa'i kha dog dang ldan pa/dge rgyas dang /'bras bu che ba dang /gnas gtsang ba'i ris kyi lha rnams kyis kyang shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa la 
7a6('bum): de ltar spyod pa'i byang chub kyi sems dpa' sems dpa'  chen po de la phyag bya bar 'gyur ro//rab 'byor phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams na/de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par 
7a7('bum): rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas grangs med/tshad med pa gang ji snyed cig cig bzhugs shing 'tsho skyong ba de dag kyang byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi pa la spyod pa 
7a8('bum): de la dgongs pa mdzad de/byang chub sems dpa' semasadapa' chen po shes rab kyi phyin pa la spyod pa de ni/sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa yongasu rdzogs par byed pa'o//tshul khrims 
7b1('bum): kyi pha rol tu phyin pa yongs su rdzogs par byed pa'o//bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa yongasu rdzogs par byed pa'o/brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa yongasasu rdzogs par byed pa'o//bsam
7b2('bum): gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa yongs rdzogs par byed pa'o/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa yong su rdzogs par byed pa'o/nang stong pa nyid yongs su rdzogs par byed ba'o//phyi stong pa nyid
7b3('bum): yongasu rdzogs par byed pa'o //phyi nang stong pa nyid yongasu rdzogs par byed pa'//stong pa nyid stong pa nyid yongasu rdzogs 
7b4('bum): don dam pa stong pa nyid yongasu rdzogs bar byed pa'o//'dus byas stong pa nyid yongs su rdzoms par byed pa'o//'dus ma byas stong pa nyid yongs du rdzogs par byed pa'o/
7b5('bum): mtha' las 'das pa stong pa nyid yongasu rjogs par byed pa'o/thog ma dang tha ma med pa stong pa nyid yongasu rdzos par byed pa'o//dor ba med pa stong pa nyid yongs su rdzos par byed
7b6('bum): pa'o/rang bzhin stong pa nyid yongasu rdzogs phar byed pa'o//chos thams cad stong pa nyid yongasu rdzog par byed pa'o/rang gi mtshan nyid stong pa nyid yongs su rjogs par byed pa'o//mi dmigs
7b7('bum): pa stong pa nyid yongasu rdzogags par byed pa'o//dngos po med pa stong pa nyid yong su rdzogs par byed pa'o//ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid yongasu rdzogs par byed pa'o//dngosapo med pa'i ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid
7b8('bum): yongasu rdzogs par byed pa'o//dran pa nye bar gzhag pa bzhi yongasu rdzogs par byed do//yang dag par spong ba bzhi yongasu rdzogs par bye ba'o//rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa bzhi yongasu rdzogs par
8a1('bum): @#//byed pa'o//dbang po lnga yongasu rdzogs par byed pa'o//stobs lnga yongasu rdzogs par byed ba'o//byang chub kyi yan lag bdun yongasu rdzogs par byed pa'i//
8a2('bum): 'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad pa yongs su rdzogs par byed pa'o/'phags pa'i bden pa bzhi yongasu rdzogs par byed pa 'o//bsam gtan bzhi yongasu rdzogs par bye pa'o//tshad 
8a3('bum): med pa bzhi yongasu rdzogs par byaid pa'o//gzugs med pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa bzhi yongs su rdzogs par byedapa'o//rnam par thar pa brgyad yongasu rdzogs par byed pa'//mthar 
8a4('bum): gyis gnas pa'i snyoms par 'jug par dgu yongasu rdzogs par byed pa//rnam par thar pa'i sgo stong pa nyid dang /mtshan ma med pa dang /smon pa med pa yongs su rdzogs par byaid pa'o //
8a5('bum): mngon par shes pa rnams yongs su rdzogs par byed ba'o//tinge 'dzin rnams yongs su rdzogs pa byed pa'o//gzungs kyi sgo rnams yongasu rdzogs par byed pa'o//de bzhin 
8a6('bum): gshegs pa'i stobs bcu yongasu rdzogs par byed pa'o //mi 'jigs pa bzhi yongs su rdzogs bar byed pa'o//so so yang dag par rig pa bzhi yongasu rdzogs par byid pa'o//snying rje 
8a7('bum): chen po yongasu rdzogs par byed pa 'o//sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bco brgyad yongasu rdzogs par byaid pa'o//rgyun tu zhugs pa'i 'bras bu yongasu rdzogs par byed ba'o//lan cig 
8a8('bum): phyir 'ong ba'i 'bras bu yongs su rdzogs par byedapa'o//phyir mi 'ong ba'i 'bras bu yongs du rdzogs par byed pa'o//dgra bcom pa nyid yongasu rdzogs par byed pa'//rang byang chub yongasu 
8b1('bum): rdzogs par byed pa'o//lam gyi rnam pa shes pa nyid yongs su rdzogs par byed pa'o//rnam pa thams cad mkhyed pa nyid yongasu rdzogs par byed pa'o/zhes dgongs pa mdzad do//rab 'byor 
8b2('bum): byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod pa ni/gang la sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis dgongs pa mdzad pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems
8b3('bum): chen po de ni sangs rgyas su 'gyur ba bzhugs par rig par bya'o//rab 'byor gang+gA'i klung gi bye ma snyed kyi 'jig rten gi khams dag na sems can gang ji snyed cig yod pa de dag thams cad bdud 
8b4('bum): sdig to can du gyur te/bdud sdig to can re res kyang de snyed kyi bdud sdig to can mngon par sprul kyang /de dag thams cad kyis byang chub sems dpa' sem dpa' chen po de la bar 
8b5('bum): chad bya bar mi nuso//rab 'byor chos gnyis dang ldan pa'i bya nga chub sems dpa' sems pa' chen po ni bdud sdig to ca na rnams kyis mi thub pa yin te/gnyis gang zhe na/chos 
8b6('bum): thams cad stong ba'o zhes bya bar rnam par bltas pa yin/des sems can thams cad kyang yongs su ma btang ba yin te/rab 'byor chos 'di gnyis dang ldan pa'i byang chub sems dpa'i sems 
8b7('bum): dpa' chen po ni bdud sdigs to can rnams kyis mi thub pa yi no//rab 'byor gzhan yang shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po chos gnyis dang 
8b8('bum): ldan pa'i bdud sdig to can rnams kyis mi thubapa yin te/gnyis gang zhen/ji skad du smras pa de bzhin du byed pa yin/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis dgongs pa yin te/rab 
9a1('bum): @#//'byor shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen pos chos de gnyis dang ldan pa ni bdud sdig to can rnams kyis mi thub pa yin no /
9a2('bum): rab 'byor de ltar spyod pa'i byang chub sems pa' sems dpa' chen po'i drung du ni lha'i bu rnams kyang 'ong bar sems so//bsnyen bkur ba dang yongasu 'dri ba dang /kun 'dri ba yang byed par 
9a3('bum): 'gyur te/rigs kyi bu khyod sangs rgyas kyi byang chub myur du thob par 'gyur gyis/de bas na rigs kyi bu khyod kyis 'di lta ste/stong pa nyid kyis rnam par spyod pa dang /mtshan ma med pas 
9a4('bum): rnam par spyod pa dang /smon pa med pas rnam par spyod pa de nyid kyis rnam par spyod par gyis shig/de ci'i phyir zhe na/rigs kyi bu khyod kyis rnam par spyod pa des/rnam par 
9a5('bum): spyod pa dang sems can mgon med pa rnams kyi 'gon du 'gyur ro//sems can skyabs med pa rnams kyi skyabs su 'gyuro//sems can dpung gnyen med pa rnams kyi dpung 
9a6('bum): gnyen du 'gyur ro//sems cad gnas med pa rnams kyi gnasu 'gyuro//sems can rten med pa rnams kyi rten du 'gyur ro//sems cang gling med pa rnams kyi gling du 'gyur ro//sems
9a7('bum): can mun par gyur pa rnams kyi snang bar 'gyur ro zhes de la spro ba bskyed par 'gyur ro//de ci'i phyir zhe na/de ni 'di ltar 'jig rten gyi khams tshad med/grangs med pa dag na/sangs rgyas

15b5('bum): gang chen po stong pa nyid la bsgom pa'i lta ci smros/don dam pa stong pa nyid kyang mi dmiga' na/gang don dam pa stong pa nyid la bsgom pa' lta ci smros/'dus byas stong pa

15b7('bum): mtha' las 'das pa stong pa nyid kyang mi migs na/gang mtha' las 'das pa stong ba nyid la bsgom pa lta ci smos/thog ma dang tha ma med pa stong pa nyid kyang mi gas na/gang 
15b8('bum): thog ma dang tha ma med pastong pa nyid la bsgom pa lta ci smos/dor ba med pa stong pa nyid kyang ma dmigs na/gang dor ba med pa stong pa nyis la bsgom pa lta ci smos/rang bzhin stong pa nyid 
16a1('bum): @#//kyang mi dmigs na/gang rang bzhin stong pa nyid la sgom pa lta ci smos/chos thams cad stong pa nyid kyang mi dmigs na/gang chos thams cad stong pa nyid

16b1('bum): 'phags pa'i bden pa rnams kyang mi dmigs na/gang 'phags pa'i bden pa rnamasal sgom pa lta ci smos/bsam gtan rnams kyang mi dmigs na/gang bsam gtan rnams la 
16b2('bum): sgom pa lta ci smos/tshad med pa rnams kyang mi dmigs na/gang tshad med pa rnams la sgom ba lta ci smos/gzugs med pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa rnams kyang mi dmigs /

260b3('bum): dkrug par mi bya'o//'ad dag ni mngon par shes pa rnamaso//'di ni mngon par shes pa rnams kyi chos nyid do ces rnam par dkrug par ma bya'o//'di dag ni ting nge 'dzin rnams so//'di ni ting nge
260b4('bum): 'dzin rnams kyi chos nyid do zhe rnam par dkrug par mi ba 'o//'di dag ni gzungs kyi sgo rnams so//'di ni gzungs kyi sgo rnams kyi chos nyid do zhesarnam par dkrug par mi 
260b5('bum): 'di dag ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs bcu'o//'di ni de bzhin gshegs pa'istobs bcu'i chos nyid do zhes rnam par
260b6('bum): 'di ni mi 'jigs pa rnams kyi chos nyid no zhes rnam par dkrug par mi bya'o//'di dag ni so so yang dag par rig pa bzhi'o//'di ni so so yang dag par rang rig pa rnams kyi chos nyid do/zhes rnam par
260b7('bum): dkrug par mi bya'o//'di ni byams pa chen po'o//'di ni byams pa pa chen po'i chos nyid do zhes rnam pa rang dkrug par mi bya'o///'di ni snying rje chen po'o//'di ni snying rje chenapo'i chos nyid do zhe
260b8('bum): rnam par dkrug par mi bya 'o//'di dag ni sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bco brgyad do//'di ni sangs rgyas kyi chos la 'dres pa rnams kyi chos nyid do zhe rnam par dkrug par mi ba'o//
261a1('bum): @#//'di ni rgyun du zhugs pa'i 'bras bu'o//'di ni rgyun du zhugasapa'i 'bras bu'i chos nyid do zhes rnam par dkrug par mi bya'o//'di ni lan cig phyir 'ong ba'i 'bras bu'o//
261a2('bum): 'di ni lan cig phyir 'ong pa'i 'bras bu'i chos nyid do/zhes rnam par dkrug par myi bya'o//'di ni phyir mi 'ong ba'i 'bras bu'o//'di ni phyir mi 'ong pa'i 'bras bu'i chos nyad do zhes rnam par dkrugs par 
261a3('bum): mi bya'o//'di ni dgra bcom pa nyid do//'di ni dgra bcom pa nyad kyi chos nyid do zhes rnam pa dkrug par mi bya'o/'di ni rang byang chub bo//'di ni rang byang chub kyi chos nyid do zhes rnams par dkrug 
261a4('bum): par mi bya'o//'di ni lam gyi rnam pa shes pa nyid do//'di ni lam gyi rnam shes pa nyid kyi chos nyid do zhes rnamapar dkrug par mi bya'o//'di ni rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid do//
261a5('bum): 'di ni rnam pa thams cad 'khyen pa nyid kyi chos nyid do ces rnam par dkrug par ma bya'o//gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das gal te chos rnams kyi chos nyid rnam par dkrug par mi bgyid pa 
261a6('bum): lags na/de la de bzhin gshegs pas 'di ni gzugs so//'di ni tshor ba'o//'di ni 'du shes so//'di ni 'du byed do//'di ni rnam par shes so//'di ni mig go/'di ni rna ba'o//'di ni 
261a7('bum): sna'o//'di ni lce'o//'di ni lus so//'di ni yid do//'di ni gzugs so//'dis ni sgra'o//'di ni dri'o//'di ni ro'o//'di ni reg go/'di ni chos so//'di ni mig gi rnam par shes pa'o//
261a8('bum): 'di ni rna ba'i rnam par shes pa'o//'di ni sna'i rnam par shes pa'o//'id ni lce'i rnam par shes pa'o//'di ni lus kyi rnam par shes pa'o//'ad ni yid kyi rnam par shes pa'o/'di ni mig 
261b1('bum): gi 'dus te reg pa'o//'di ni rna ba'i 'dus te reg pa'o//'dir ni sna'i 'dus te reg pa'o//'di ni lce'i 'dus te reg pa'o/'di ni lus kyi 'dus te reg pa'o//'ad ni yid kyi 'dus te reg pa'o/
261b2('bum): 'di ni mig gi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba'o//'di ni rna ba'i 'dusata reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba'o//'di ni sna'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba'o//'di ni lce'i 'dus te regapa'i rkyen gyis 
261b3('bum): tshor ba'o//'di ni lus kyi 'dus te rig pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba'o //'di ni yid kyi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba'o/'di ni sa'i khams so//'di ni chu'i khams so//'di ni me'i khams so//
261b4('bum): 'di ni rlung gi khams so//'di ni nam mkha'i khams so//'di ni rnam par shes pa'i khams so//'di ni ma rig pa'o//'di ni 'du byed do//'di ni rnam par shesapa'o//'di ni ming dang gzugs
261b5('bum): so;;'di ni skye mched drug go/'di ni reg pa'o//'di ni tsho ra ba'o//'di ni sred pa'o//'di ni len pa'o//'di ni srid pa'o//'di ni skye ba'o//'di ni rga shi'o//'di ni sbyin
261b6('bum): pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa'o//'di ni tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'o//'di ni bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa'o//'di ni brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'o//'di ni bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu 
261b7('bum): phyin pa'o//'di ni shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'o/'di ni nang stong pa nyid do//'di ni phyi stong pa nyid do//'di ni phyi nang stong pa nyid do/'di ni stong pa nyid stong pa nyid do//'dir ni chen po stong pa 
261b8('bum): nyid do//'di ni don dam pa stong pa nyid do//'di na 'dus byas stong pa nyid do//'di ni 'dus ma byas stong pa nyid do//'dir ni mtha' las 'das pa stong pa nyid do//'di ni thog ma dang tha ma med pa stong pa 
262a1('bum): @#//nyid do//'di ni dor ba med pa stong pa nyid do//'di ni rang bzhin stong pa nyid do//'di nichos thams cad stong pa nyid do//'di nirang gi mtshan nyid stong pa nyid do//'dir ni mi 
262a2('bum): dmigs pa stong pa nyid do//'dini dngos po med pa stong pa nyid do//'di ni ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid do/'di ni dngos po med pa'i ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid dngo//'di dag ni dran pa nye bar gzhag pa rnamaso//
262a3('bum): 'di dag ni yang dag par spong ba rnams so//'di dag ni rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa rnams so//'di dag nidabang po rnamaso//'di dag ni stobs rnams so//'di dag ni/byang chub kyi yan lag rnams so//
262a4('bum): 'di dag ni 'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad pa'o//'di dag ni 'phags pa'i bden pa rnamaso//'di dag ni bsam gtan rnams so//'di dag nitshad med pa rnamaso//'di dag
262a5('bum): ni gzugs med pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa rnams so//'di dgani rnam par thar pa brgyad do//'di dag ni mthar gyis gnas pa'i snyoms par 'jug par dgu'o//'di dag
262a6('bum): ni rnam par thar pa'i sgo stong pa nyid dang /mtshan ma med dang /smon pa med pa'o//'di dag ni mngon par shes pa rnams so//'di dag na tinge 'dzin rnams so//'di dag mi gzungs kyi sgo rnamaso/
262a7('bum): 'di dag ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs bcu'o//'di dag ni mi 'jigs pa bzhi'o//'di dag ni so so yang dag par rig pa bzhi'o//'di ni byams pa chen po'o//'di ni snying rje chen po'o/

262b1('bum): dgra bcom pa nyid do//'di ni rang byang chub bo//'di ni lam gyi rnam pa shes pa nyid do//'di ni rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid do//zhes rnam par dkrug par mdzad na/de'i slad du bcom ldan 
262b2('bum): 'das kyis chos rnams kyi chos nyid rnam par dkrug par ma gyur tam/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/rab 'byor de ni ma yin no//ngas ji ltar na gzhan rnams khong du chud par 'gyur 
262b3('bum): zhes ming dang mtshan mas chos rnams kyi chos nyidabastan to//chos rnams brjod kyi chos nyid rnam par dkug pa med do//gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das gal te gzhan rnams khong du chud
262b4('bum): par bgyi ba'i slad du/bcom ldan 'das kyis ming dang mtshan mas chosarnams kyi chos nyid bstan te/chos rnams brjod pa mdzad na/bcom ldan 'das chos thams cad ming 
262b5('bum): ma mchis shing mtshan ma mchis pa la/ji ltar ming dang mtshan mas brjod pa mdzad na//bcom ldan 'das kyis/bka' stsal pa/rab 'byorabarjod pa ni ming ma yin/
262b6('bum): mtshan ma yang ma yin/mngon par chags pa yang ma yin te/rab 'byor gzhan du na sdug bsngal nyid brjod par zad de/ming dang mtshan ma la mngon par chags pa med do//rab 'byor de bzhin 
262b7('bum): gshegs pa 'am/de bzhin gshegs pa'i nyan thos ni ming dang mtshan ma la mngonapar chags par mi 'gyur te/rab 'byor gal te ming la mngon par chags par 'gyur ram/mngon par 
262b8('bum): chags par 'gyur ba ni/stong pa nyid kyang stong pa nyid la mngon par chags par gyur ro//mtshan ma med pa yang mtshan ma med pa la mngon par chags par 'gyur ro//smon pa med pa yang 
263a1('bum): @#//smon pa med pa la mngon par chags par 'gyur ro//de bzhin nyid kyang de bzhin nyid la mngon par chags par gyur ro//yang dag pa'i mtha' yang yang dag pI 
263a2('bum): mtha' la mngon par chags par 'gyur ro//chos kyi dbyings kyang chos kyi dbyings la mngon par chags par 
263a3('bum): te;chos 'di dag thams cad kyang ming tsam du zad pas na de dag ni ming tsam la gnas med do//rab 'byor de ltar byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen pos ming dang mtshan ma tsam la gnas 
263a4('bum): te;shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyad par bya ste/de la mngon par chags par mi bya'o//gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das gal te de ltar 'dus byas 'di dag thams cad ming 
263a5('bum): dang mtshan ma tsam du bas pa lags na/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po de dag gang gi don tu byang chub tu sems bskyed cing /byang chub tu sems bskyed nas/
263a6('bum): 'du byed rnam pa du ma nyams su myong bar bgyid pa dang /byang chub sems dpa'i spyad pa spyod pa lags/byang chub sems dpa'i spyad pa spyod cing sbyin pa sna tshogs yongasu gtong ba dang /tshul 
263a7('bum): khrims bsrung ba dang /bzod pa bsgrub pa dang /brtson 'grus rtsom pa dang /ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par 'jug pa dang /shes rab bsgom pa dang /nang stong pa nyid la spyod pa dang /phyi stong pa 
263a8('bum): nyid la spyod pa dang /phyi nang stong pa nyid la spyod pa dang /stong pa nyid stong pa nyid la spyod pa dang /chen po stong pa nyid la spyod pa danga /don dam pa stong pa nyid la spyod pa da nga /'dus byas stong pa 
263b1('bum): nyid la spyod pa dang /'dus ma byas stong pa nyid la spyod pa dang /mtha' las 'das pa stong pa nyid la spyod pa dag/thog ma dang tha ma med pa stong pa nyid la spyod pa'i dang /dor ba med pa stong pa nyid
263b2('bum): la spyod pa dang /rang bzhin stong pa nyid la spyod pa dan/chos thams cad stong pa nyid la spyod pa rang /rang gi mtshan nyid stong pa nyid la spyod pa dadang /mi dmigs pa stong pa nyid la spyodapa dang /
263b3('bum): dngos po med pa stong pa nyid la spyad pa dang /ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid la spyod dang /dngos po med pa'i ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid las spyod pa dang /dran pa nye bar gzhag pa rnams la spyod pa dang /yang dag
263b4('bum): par spong ba rnams la spyod pa dang /rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa rnams la spyod pa dang/dbang po rnams la spyod pa dang /stobs rnams la spyod pa dang /byang chub kyi yan lag rnams la spyod
263b5('bum): pa dang /'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad pa la spyod pa dang /'phags pa'i bden pa bzhi la spyod pa dang /bsam gtan bzhi la spyod pa dang /tshad med pa bzhi la spyod
263b6('bum): pa dang /gzugs med pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa bzhi la spyod pa dang/rnam par thar pa brgyad la spyod pa dang /mthar gyis gnas pa'i snyoms par 'jug par 'jug pa dgu la spyod pa dang /
263b7('bum): rnam par thar pa'i sgo stong pa nyid dang /mtshan ma med pa dang /smon pa med pa la spyod pa dang /mngon par shes pa rnams la spyod pa dang ting nge 'dzin rnams la spyod pa dang gzungs kyi sgo rnams la
263b8('bum): spyod pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs bcu la spyod pa dang /mi 'jigs pa bzhi la spyod pa dang so so yang dag par rig pa bzhi la spyod pa dang byams pa chen po la spyod pa dang snying rje
264a1('bum): @#//chen po la spyod pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bco brgyad la spyod pa lags/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/rab 'byor gang de skad du gal te 'dus

264a3('bum): 'dus byas thams cad ming dang mtshon ma tsam du zad do//ming yang ming gis stong /mtshan ma yang mtshan mas stong bas na/de'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' sem dpa' chen po byang chub sems 
264a4('bum): dpa'i spyad pa spyod cing rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid thob par byed do//rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa'i nyid thob nas /chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ro//chos kyi 'khor lo bskor 
264a5('bum): nas sems can rnams theg pa gsum gyis yongs su mya ngan las zlas te/ming dang mtshan ma de la skye ba dang /'jig pa dang /gnas pa dang /gnas pa las

264a8('bum): gsungs pa dang /lam gyi rnam pa shes pa nyid ces mdor gsungs pa dang /rnam pa thams cad mkhyen panyid kyang de bzhin gshegs pas gsungs na/bcom ldan 'das thams cad mkhyen 
264b1('bum): pa de gsum la khyad ni ci zhig mchis/bye brag ni ci zhig mchis/rnam pa ni ci zhig mchis/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/rab 'byor thams cad shes pa nyid ces bya ba ni nyan

264b6('bum): yang 'di skad du ci'i phyir na/byang chub sems dpa' semasad pa' chen po rnams la lam gyi rnam pa nyid ces bya zhes zer ba ni/rab 'byor lam thams cad byang chub sems dpa' 
264b7('bum): sems dpa' chen pos bsgrub par bya/lam thams cad byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen pos shes par bya ste/nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas kyi lam gang ji snyed pa

265a1('bum): @#//mtha' mngon du mi byed pa'i phyir ro//gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das de ltar byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen pos sangs rgyas kyi lam yongasu rdzogs

265a4('bum): byang chub sems dpa' semasadapa' chen pos lam la gnas te/yang dag pa'i mtha' mngon du bgyi 'am/bcom ma ldan 'das kyis bka' rtsal pa/ma yin no//gsol

265b2('bum): la lam ma yin pa la yang gnas te 'am/gsol ba/bcom ldan 'das de ni la lagaso//bcom ldan 'das kyi bka' stsal pa/'o na la ma ma yin la lam ma yin pa la yang ma yin

266a1('bum): @#//pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid ces bgyi ba thams cad mkhyen pa nyid 'di gsum las nyon mongs pa spangs pa'i rnam pa 'dis ni lhag ma ma lus par spangs so//'dis ni lhag

266b1('bum): pa yang gnod par 'gyur ba ni ma yin te/de ni de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams la thams cad du thams cad mi mnga' zhing /mi dmigs so//de
